--- the ending ----------------------------------------------------------
  The silt comes away in one piece, the way a thing does when it has been there long enough to have set, and the door opens inwards on four hinges of which two are original.
  Behind it is a landing and a stair going down, cut for people carrying something, and the stair does not stop where the light does.
  This is where the writing stops. Not the game — the writing. What is at the bottom of that stair is not hidden and not locked and not saved for later: nobody has decided it yet.
  You dropped a tower, drained a lake and walked two hundred and six steps into the dark, and the last fact this game has for you is that somebody cut them.
